1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the microevolution structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 760. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 141 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 760 + O(1/n^141). Summing over all modes and applying the microevolution convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)
